FROM "Books" AS "b"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "c"."Id", "c"."ConcurrencyToken", "c"."CreatedAt", "c"."Description", "c"."IsDeleted", "c"."Name", "c"."UpdatedAt"
    FROM "Categories" AS "c"
    WHERE NOT ("c"."IsDeleted")
) AS "c0" ON "b"."CategoryId" = "c0"."Id"
WHERE NOT ("b"."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY "c0"."Name"
